Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the atoms space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 124 and the convergence rate is O(255/n^4).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 124 + O(1/n^255). Summing over all modes and applying the atoms convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
